# Tonic occurrence review (Jev-assisted)

Generated from `~/ov-grpc-logs/jev-v2-answers.jsonl` (verbatim Jev responses + exact request payloads; API key never logged).
Model: `jev-1.14.0` via `POST https://api.typesafe.ai/v1/systemone`. One request per occurrence (parallel-questions cookbook), 6 concurrent.

Per occurrence Jev was asked 9 atomic **Nouls** (judgments), 1 **Choice** over replacement options (classification-using-confidence: apply only if confidence ≥ 0.9),
and 2 **verify Nouls** on a code-computed proposed replacement (verify-LLM-outputs: risk = 1 − supported, review if ≥ 0.2). The decision is made in code (`decide()` in `jev_review_v2.py`).

Scope: every line matching `tonic` in `crates/` and the root `Cargo.toml` (excluding `target/`, `*.md`, `crates/_3tched-fs/**`). The two checked-in op-chat generated files are one item each.
The grpc 0.9 facts given to Jev were taken from the crate source (see docs/grpc-migration-plan.md); Jev's answers are judgments over those facts, not independent verification.

Items: 610 · errors: 0 · flagged: 511 (high-priority: 122)

| decision (code) | count |
|---|---|
| kept tonic 0.14 | 419 |
| left untouched (not code) | 98 |
| kept tonic 0.14 (adapted) | 73 |
| left untouched | 20 |

## How the answers were applied

* **No grpc replacement was applied to existing code.** No occurrence met the code rule for a grpc move
  (Choice `grpc_*` with confidence ≥ 0.9 **and** `grpc_has_equivalent` ≥ 0.7 **and** not tonic-type/server-bound).
  Jev put `grpc_has_equivalent` ≥ 0.7 on **0 of 512** code occurrences; 122 occurrences had a `grpc_*` top choice,
  all low confidence (≤ 0.75) and all listed above for human review. The only grpc code is the new opt-in
  `op-grpc-bridge` feature `grpc-preview` (`src/grpc_preview.rs`), which adds a client path and replaces nothing.
* **Phase 1 (tonic 0.12 → 0.14) edits are compiler-forced, not Jev-chosen.** The version bump itself was
  requested, and grpc 0.9 requires tonic 0.14. The 73 "kept tonic 0.14 (adapted)" lines are the minimal source changes
  that bump forces (`tonic_build` → `tonic_prost_build`, `BoxBody`/`empty_body`/`boxed` → `Body`, `tonic_web::enable`
  → `GrpcWebLayer`, TLS feature renames, axum 0.8 router mounted under axum 0.7). Jev's top Choice for them
  was `keep_tonic_014`, but below the 0.9 confidence bar, so **all 73 stay flagged for human review** even though they
  build and the bridge tests pass.
* Jev Noul summary over the 512 code occurrences (mean / count ≥ 0.7): names a tonic-only type 0.87 / 445,
  interceptors 0.05 / 6, transport TLS 0.05 / 8, codegen 0.26 / 59, removed/changed in 0.14 0.19 / 54,
  grpc has an equivalent 0.15 / 0.
* Only 38 Choices reached confidence ≥ 0.9 (32 `leave_untouched`, 6 `keep_tonic_014`); all were honoured.
